### `SmartKitProvider` — component

`import { SmartKitProvider } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/context';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `services` | **yes** | `Record&lt;string, ServiceConfig>` | Services by name: `{ model, dataProvider, variants? }` each, usually from `loadService({ serviceUrl, fetch })` of `@rhfan2019/smart-kit-core`. Required. |
| `locale` | **yes** | `Locale` | UI language of the library texts and number / date formats: "en" or "zh". Required. |
| `namespace` | **yes** | `string` | Prefix of every page state key and query key, e.g. "my-app". |
| `defaultService` |  | `string` | Service used when a building block does not name one. Defaults to the first service. |
| `messages` |  | `Partial&lt;Messages>` | Overrides of library texts. |
| `timeZone` |  | `string` | IANA time zone for Edm.DateTimeOffset display and filtering. Defaults to the browser's zone. |
| `navigation` |  | `NavigationAdapter` | Connects the library to the host router: `toObject`, `toList`, `href`, `hasObjectPage`, `toTable`. Without it rows do not navigate. |
| `pageState` |  | `PageStateStore` | Where page state (filters, sort, scroll) is kept for back navigation; default in memory. `createSessionPageStateStore()` keeps it in sessionStorage. |
| `renderers` |  | `RendererRegistry` | Custom renderers for fields by type name or property (white-box extension point). |
| `queryClient` |  | `QueryClient` | TanStack Query client; default: one created by the provider. |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode` | The application (pages using smart-kit building blocks). |

### `SmartKitProviderProps` — interface

Props of `SmartKitProvider` (see above).

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/i18n`

`createText` (function) · `INTL_LOCALE` (const) · `Locale` (type) · `MessageKey` (type) · `Messages` (type) · `MESSAGES` (const) · `TextFn` (type) · `TextParams` (type) · `TypedKey` (type) · `typedText` (function)

### `createText` — function

```ts

```

### `INTL_LOCALE` — const

```ts
const INTL_LOCALE: Record<Locale, string>
```

### `Locale` — type

```ts
export type Locale = 'zh' | 'en';
```

### `MessageKey` — type

```ts
export type MessageKey = keyof Messages;
```

### `Messages` — type

The library texts; the control texts (docs/26) are loaded per control group, but can be overridden here too.

```ts
export type Messages = Widen<typeof en & typeof controlsEn>;
```

### `MESSAGES` — const

The library texts per locale; the control texts are not included (they come with each control group, docs/26).

```ts
const MESSAGES: Record<Locale, Widen<{ readonly 'filterBar.search': "Search"; readonly 'filterBar.go': "Go"; readonly 'filterBar.adaptFilters': { readonl…
```

### `TextFn` — type

```ts
export type TextFn = (key: MessageKey, params?: TextParams) => string;
```

### `TextParams` — type

```ts
export type TextParams = Record<string, string | number>;
```

### `TypedKey` — type

Keys that have a variant `<key>Type` naming the kind of record (UI.HeaderInfo/TypeName).

```ts
export type TypedKey = 'create.done' | 'delete.done' | 'edit.conflict' | 'object.notFound' | 'object.loadError';
```

### `typedText` — function

The message naming the record type ("用户已创建。"); without a type name the neutral text ("已创建。").

```ts
typedText(t: TextFn, key: TypedKey, typeName: string | undefined, params?: TextParams): string
```

### `DateLike` — type

A date property of the controls: a Date or an ISO string (UI5 accepts a string or a Date).

```ts
export type DateLike = Date | string;
```

### `DEFAULT_STANDARD_OPTIONS` — const

The default `standardOptions` of 1.154 (all 68 keys, in the UI5 order).

```ts
const DEFAULT_STANDARD_OPTIONS: readonly ("DATE" | "TODAY" | "YESTERDAY" | "TOMORROW" | "FIRSTDAYWEEK" | "LASTDAYWEEK" | "FIRSTDAYMONTH" | "LASTDAYMONTH" | "FIRSTDAYQUAR…
```

### `dynamicDateValueTypes` — function

The value types of an option key (UI5 `DynamicDateOption.getValueTypes` of the standard options).

```ts
dynamicDateValueTypes(key: string): string[]
```

### `ExtendEntry` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | **yes** | `string` | Id of the new item (insert) or of the item to replace. |
| `position` | **yes** | `ExtendPosition` |  |
| `item` | **yes** | `T \| ((original: T) => T)` | New item (insert) or replacement (replace). For replace, a function receives the original. |
| `anchor` |  | `string` | Id of the item to insert before or after. |

### `ExtendPosition` — type

Id-based replace / insert for model-generated items. See docs/06-extension-and-theming.md §1.

```ts
export type ExtendPosition = 'before' | 'after' | 'replace' | 'start' | 'end';
```

### `messageTypeOf` — function

core `Message.type` (lower case, ADR-023) → MessageType.

```ts
messageTypeOf(type: Message["type"]): MessageType
```

### `PreventableEvent` — type

An event object for handlers whose UI5 event can be prevented (`oEvent.preventDefault()`).

```ts
export type PreventableEvent<T> = T & { preventDefault(): void };
```

### `RESTRICTED_FILE_NAME_CHARACTERS` — const

Characters not allowed in file names by default (UI5).

```ts
const RESTRICTED_FILE_NAME_CHARACTERS: "\\:/*?\"<>|[]{}@#$"
```

### `toDates` — function

The dates of a value (sap.m.DynamicDateRange.toDates): start of the first day and end of the last day in local time; FROM / TO give one date, as in UI5. `now` is an internal hook for tests.

```ts
toDates(value: DynamicDateRangeValue, calendarWeekNumbering?: CalendarWeekNumbering, now?: Date): Date[]
```

### `usePageState` — hook

```ts
usePageState<T>(key: string | undefined, initial: T | (() => T)): [T, (next: T | ((prev: T) => T)) => void]
```

"Filtered by: Status (New, Approved), Region (North)".

```ts
viewSettingsFilterString(filterItems: ViewSettingsFilterItem[], filters: Record<string, string[]>, t: TextFn): string
```

### `RendererRegistry` — type

Keys, from most to least specific: - `path:<EntityType full name>/<path>` — one field - `rating`, `progress`, `image`, `link`, `currency`, `unit`, `criticality`, `boolean` - `type:<Edm type>`, e.g. `type:Edm.Date`

```ts
export type RendererRegistry = Record<string, FieldRenderer>;
```

### `SmartField` — component

`import { SmartField } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/field';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `row` | **yes** | `Row \| undefined` | The row to show the value of. Required (undefined while loading). Text, unit, criticality and links come from the annotations of the property. |
| `typeName` |  | `string` | Entity type full name the field belongs to. Use `entitySet` instead when convenient. |
| `entitySet` |  | `string` | Entity set the row belongs to (alternative to `typeName`). |
| `service` |  | `string` | Name of the service in `SmartKitProvider` `services`; default: the provider's `defaultService`. |
| `field` |  | `FieldModel` | A field from the model, or just a property path. |
| `path` |  | `string` | Property path, e.g. "GrossAmount" or "_Owner/Name" (alternative to `field`). |
| `mode` |  | `'display' \| 'edit'` | "display" (default): formatted value with text arrangement, unit, criticality; "edit": the editor for the Edm type (value help from `Common.ValueList`). |
| `value` |  | `unknown` | Edit mode: controlled value and change handler. |
| `onChange` |  | `(value: unknown) => void` | Edit mode: called with the new value. |
| `aria-label` |  | `string` | Label for accessibility when used without a visible label. |
| `error` |  | `string` | Edit mode: error text, mandatory marker, read-only, element id. |
| `required` |  | `boolean` | Edit mode: mandatory marker. |
| `readOnly` |  | `boolean` | Edit mode: show the value read-only. |
| `id` |  | `string` | Edit mode: element id of the input. |
| `showLabel` |  | `boolean` | Edit mode: show the label above the editor (dialogs). |
| `cell` |  | `boolean` | Rendered in a table cell: a micro chart without data leaves the cell empty (docs/15 MC-06). |

### `ConditionInput` — component

`import { ConditionInput } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/filter';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `entitySet` | **yes** | `string` | Entity set of the filtered property. |
| `path` | **yes** | `string` | Filtered property path, e.g. "Status". |
| `conditions` | **yes** | `Condition[]` | Conditions of the field (controlled). |
| `onChange` | **yes** | `(conditions: Condition[]) => void` | Called with the new conditions (typed values become "EQ" conditions; "=x", ">x", "a...b", "*x*" are parsed). |
| `label` | **yes** | `string` | Accessible name, usually the property label. |
| `service` |  | `string` | Name of the service in `SmartKitProvider` `services`; default: the provider's `defaultService`. |
| `error` |  | `string` | Error text shown under the field. |
| `required` |  | `boolean` | Shows the mandatory marker and validates that a value is entered. |
| `onValueHelp` |  | `() => void` | Opens the value help dialog (rendered by the caller). |
| `id` |  | `string` | Element id (e.g. for an external `&lt;label htmlFor>`). |

### `ConditionInputProps` — interface

Props of `ConditionInput` (see above).

### `conditionText` — function

Token text of a condition, e.g. "Office (C1)", ">10", "!(=X)", "This month".

```ts
conditionText(c: Condition, prop: PropertyModel | undefined, locale: string, timeZone?: string, options?: ConditionTextOptions): string
```

### `ConditionTextOptions` — interface

Options of `conditionText`.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `t` |  | `TextFn` | Texts for relative dates ("This month", "Last 7 days"); without it the operator name is shown ("THISMONTH"). |
| `textArrangement` |  | `TextArrangement` | How a selected value's text and code are shown (UI.TextArrangement); default: the property's, else TextFirst. |

### `FilterStateApi` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `entitySet` | **yes** | `string` |  |
| `liveMode` | **yes** | `boolean` |  |
| `setFilters` | **yes** | `(next: FilterState) => void` |  |
| `setConditions` | **yes** | `(path: string, conditions: Condition[]) => void` |  |
| `setSearch` | **yes** | `(search: string) => void` |  |
| `setFields` | **yes** | `(fields: string[]) => void` |  |
| `go` | **yes** | `() => boolean` | Applies the current filters; returns false when mandatory fields are empty. |
| `load` | **yes** | `(content: Partial&lt;Pick&lt;FilterSnapshot, 'filters' \| 'search' \| 'fields'>>, apply: boolean) => void` | Replaces filters, search and fields (e.g. from a variant); applies them when `apply` is true. |
| `dirty` | **yes** | `boolean` | Filters differ from the applied query (manual mode shows an overlay). |
| `missingRequired` | **yes** | `string[]` | Mandatory filter paths without a complete condition. |
| `activeCount` | **yes** | `number` | Number of fields that carry a condition ("Adapt Filters (n)"). |
| `showRequiredErrors` | **yes** | `boolean` | Set when "Go" was pressed with missing mandatory fields. |
| `defaults` | **yes** | `Pick&lt;FilterSnapshot, 'filters' \| 'search' \| 'fields'>` | Defaults from the annotations (SelectionVariant, SelectionFields), i.e. the "Standard" variant. |
| `filters` | **yes** | `FilterState` |  |
| `search` | **yes** | `string` |  |
| `fields` | **yes** | `string[]` |  |
| `applied` | **yes** | `AppliedFilter \| null` | Last executed query; null until "Go" (or auto-execution) happened. |
| `service` |  | `string` |  |

### `UseFilterStateOptions` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `service` |  | `string` |  |
| `pageKey` |  | `string` | Key under which the state survives back navigation. |
| `initial` |  | `Partial&lt;Pick&lt;FilterSnapshot, 'filters' \| 'search' \| 'fields'>>` | Initial state (non-controlled). Defaults to the SelectionVariant annotation. |
| `autoExecute` |  | `boolean` | Execute on load when no mandatory field is empty (default true). |
| `liveMode` |  | `boolean` | Live update mode: apply changes automatically after a short delay (default false = manual "Go"). |
| `liveDelay` |  | `number` | Delay of the live mode in ms. |

### `SmartTable` — component

`import { SmartTable } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/table';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `service` |  | `string` | Name of the service in `SmartKitProvider` `services`; default: the provider's `defaultService`. |
| `entitySet` |  | `string` | List: entity set. |
| `parent` |  | `{ path: string; navigation: string }` | Sub-table: parent entity path and collection navigation. |
| `mode` |  | `'grid' \| 'auto' \| 'responsive'` | Table type: "grid" (AG Grid, default for lists), "responsive" (list-like rows), "auto" (responsive below 600 px; default for sub-tables). |
| `tableType` |  | `TableType` | Table type by its UI5 name (sap.ui.comp.smarttable.TableType). Without `mode` it picks the kernel: "ResponsiveTable" the responsive table, the others the grid (sub-tables: "auto"). "AnalyticalTable" is the grid table (grouping and totals come from the annotations, docs/23). "TreeTable" shows the recursive hierarchy of the entity type (Aggregation.RecursiveHierarchy, docs/26 §3.10) in the kernel of `mode`; without one it is a plain table. |
| `hierarchyQualifier` |  | `string` | Tree tables: qualifier of Aggregation.RecursiveHierarchy (default: the type's first hierarchy). |
| `initialExpansionLevel` |  | `number` | Tree tables: levels shown at first (default 1: the roots); `collapseAll` goes back to the roots. |
| `onToggleOpenState` |  | `(e: ToggleOpenStateEvent) => void` | Tree tables: a node was expanded or collapsed by the user (sap.ui.table.TreeTable#toggleOpenState). |
| `ref` |  | `Ref&lt;SmartTableRef>` | Tree tables: expand, collapse, collapseAll, expandToLevel, isExpanded (sap.ui.table.TreeTable). |
| `query` |  | `TableQuery \| null` | Applied filters; null means "not executed yet" (shows "To start, set the relevant filters."). Undefined loads without filters. |
| `columns` |  | `string[]` | Visible column ids in order (controlled) |
| `onColumnsChange` |  | `(ids: string[]) => void` | Called with the visible column ids after personalization. |
| `sort` |  | `SortItem[]` | Sort order (controlled); default from `UI.PresentationVariant/SortOrder`. |
| `onSortChange` |  | `(sort: SortItem[]) => void` | Called with the new sort order. |
| `onSelectionChange` |  | `(rows: Row[]) => void` | Called with the selected rows. |
| `onCountChange` |  | `(count: number \| undefined) => void` | Called when the total count becomes known (object page tab counts). |
| `onRowNavigate` |  | `(row: Row, path: string) => void` | Called with the row and its entity path; default: NavigationAdapter.toObject when the adapter has an object page. |
| `navigable` |  | `boolean` | Whether rows navigate (shows the chevron). Defaults to the adapter's hasObjectPage. |
| `extend` |  | `TableExtendEntry[]` | Insert, replace or remove columns and toolbar actions (ids as in the model, e.g. "DataField::GrossAmount"). |
| `title` |  | `string` | Table title in the toolbar; default `UI.HeaderInfo/TypeNamePlural`, with the row count. |
| `growingThreshold` |  | `number` | Sub-tables: initial rows (default 10) and growth (default 20). |
| `growBy` |  | `number` | Rows loaded per "More" (default 20). |
| `pageKey` |  | `string` | Key for restoring sort, columns, scroll and selection on back navigation. |
| `overlay` |  | `boolean` | Grey out the table (filters changed in manual mode). |
| `exportLimit` |  | `number` | Maximum rows of the Excel export (default 10 000). |
| `exportable` |  | `boolean` | Offer "Export to Spreadsheet" in the toolbar (default true). |
| `onBeforeExport` |  | `(e: ExportEvent) => boolean \| void \| Promise&lt;boolean \| void>` | Called when the rows to export are loaded, before the file is written. Return false (or a promise of false) to cancel: no file is written. Use it to check permissions or to log the export. |
| `onExported` |  | `(e: ExportEvent) => void` | Called after the file was written. |
| `showSearch` |  | `boolean` | Search field in the toolbar (sub-tables, when searchable). |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode` | Replace the default parts: &lt;SmartTable.Toolbar> etc. |
| `className` |  | `string` | Extra CSS class on the root element. |
| `actionGroups` |  | `ActionGroup[]` | Menu buttons for similar actions. |
| `modelActions` |  | `boolean` | Actions from UI.DataFieldForAction (default true). |
| `createMode` |  | `'dialog' \| 'inline' \| 'none'` | Create: "dialog" (list pages; LR Add/Create Using a Dialog), "inline" (a new row at the top; OP inline create), "none". Default: "dialog" for entity sets, "inline" for sub-tables. Only offered when the set is insertable and, for sub-tables, while `editing`. |
| `onCreated` |  | `(path: string, row: Row) => void` | Called after an object was created (default for lists: navigate to its object page). |
| `deletable` |  | `boolean` | Offer "Delete" for selected rows when the set is deletable (default: lists always, sub-tables while editing). |
| `editing` |  | `boolean` | Sub-tables: the parent object is in edit mode (enables Add and Delete). |
| `copy` |  | `boolean` | "Copy" of the selected rows to the clipboard (default true). |
| `growing` |  | `'button' \| 'scroll'` | "auto" mode: grow with a "More" button (default) or when the end of the table is scrolled into view. |
| `showAllThreshold` |  | `number` | "auto" mode: above this count only a preview is shown with a "Show All (x)" link (OP Tables; default 200). |
| `onShowAll` |  | `() => void` | "Show All (x)": default NavigationAdapter.toTable. |
| `titleLevel` |  | `2 \| 3 \| 4 \| 5` | Heading level and style of the title (OP Title Styles). |
| `workingMode` |  | `'worklist' \| 'continuous'` | Worklist (default): rows refresh after an action; continuous: changed rows stay and are marked (LR Working Modes). |
| `onRowsChange` |  | `(rows: Row[]) => void` | Called with the loaded rows (e.g. paging buttons of a flexible column layout). |
| `refreshToken` |  | `number` | Change this value to reload the table. |
| `lineItem` |  | `FieldModel[]` | Columns instead of UI.LineItem (multiple views with their own LineItem). |
| `titleAddon` |  | `ReactNode` | Toolbar content after the title (content switch of multiple views, LR Multiple Views). |
| `toolbarEnd` |  | `ReactNode` | Toolbar content before the settings (table / chart toggle). |
| `groupBy` |  | `string \| null` | Group column id (controlled, docs/23; grid tables then load rows in blocks of 100 while scrolling and show group header rows). Default: the column of the first `UI.PresentationVariant/GroupBy` path; null: not grouped. |
| `onGroupByChange` |  | `(id: string \| null) => void` | Called with the group column id after personalization; when given, `groupBy` is fully controlled (undefined: the default). |
| `totals` |  | `string[]` | Column ids whose totals are shown (controlled, docs/23). Default: the columns of `UI.PresentationVariant/Total`. |
| `onTotalsChange` |  | `(ids: string[]) => void` | Called with the total column ids after personalization; when given, `totals` is fully controlled (undefined: the default). |
| `presentation` |  | `PresentationModel` | Presentation variant giving the default sort, grouping and totals (multiple views); default the entity's. |

### `SmartTableRef` — interface

Instance methods of a tree table (sap.ui.table.TreeTable), through `ref`. Row indexes count the visible rows.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `expand` | **yes** | `(rowIndex: number \| number[]) => void` | Expands the node(s) at the row index(es); leaves and expanded nodes stay as they are. |
| `collapse` | **yes** | `(rowIndex: number \| number[]) => void` |  |
| `collapseAll` | **yes** | `() => void` | Collapses all nodes: only the roots stay visible. |
| `expandToLevel` | **yes** | `(level: number) => void` | Expands all nodes down to `level` (1: the roots only); single expansions and collapses are dropped. |
| `isExpanded` | **yes** | `(rowIndex: number) => boolean` |  |

| `forecast` |  | `number` | Bullet forecast (MC-23). |
| `min` |  | `number` | Bullet scale start (default: the smallest of 0 and all values, MC-26). |
| `max` |  | `number` | Bullet scale end (default: the largest value); Harvey Ball total (MaximumValue, MC-55). |
| `thresholds` |  | `MicroChartThreshold[]` | Bullet threshold markers (MC-25). |
| `mode` |  | `'actual' \| 'delta'` | Bullet: 'delta' shows the difference between the value and the target (DeltaBulletChart, MC-24). |
| `criticality` |  | `MicroChartCriticality` | Semantic colour of the value (from `UI.DataPoint/Criticality` or `CriticalityCalculation`). |
| `color` |  | `number` | Qualitative palette colour 1–11, used when there is no criticality (Harvey Ball, MC-56). Default 1. |
| `points` |  | `MicroChartPoint[]` | Series charts (column, line, comparison, area, stacked bar). Comparison shows at most 12 bars (MC-41). |
| `lines` |  | `MicroChartPoint[][]` | Line: up to three lines, one per measure (MC-35); replaces `points`. |
| `threshold` |  | `number` | Line: a threshold line (MC-39). |
| `showPoints` |  | `boolean` | Line: show the data points (MC-37; default false). |
| `areaThresholds` |  | `MicroChartAreaThresholds` | Area: threshold areas (MC-47). |
| `view` |  | `'normal' \| 'wide'` | Area: 'wide' puts the labels before and after the chart (MC-49). |
| `titles` |  | `[string, string]` | Delta: names (or dates) of the two values, the optional left labels (MC-62). |
| `columnLabels` |  | `boolean` | Column: a value label on every column when the columns are wide enough and the chart is at least 72 px high (MC-31). |
| `focusPoints` |  | `number[]` | Line: indexes of focus points of the first line, e.g. [0, n - 1] for the first and last value (MC-38). |
| `thresholdLabel` |  | `boolean` | Line: show the threshold value next to the threshold line (MC-39). |
| `minMaxLabels` |  | `boolean` | Area: the smallest and largest value as extra labels (MC-48). |
| `scaleMin` |  | `number` | Line, column, area: a fixed scale, so that the charts of a table column are comparable (MC-40). |
| `scaleMax` |  | `number` |  |
| `format` |  | `(n: number) => string` | Formats numbers for the text alternative (default Intl.NumberFormat of the locale). |
| `formatLabel` |  | `(n: number) => string` | Formats the visible number labels (default: short form of the locale, e.g. "6.7M", MC-13). |
| `hideOnNoData` |  | `boolean` | Render nothing when there is no data (table cells, MC-06); otherwise a "No data" placeholder (MC-05). |
| `size` |  | `'S' \| 'M'` |  **Deprecated**: The size follows the container (MC-02); ignored. |
| `onPress` |  | `() => void` | When set, the chart is a button (Enter / Space / click). Not used for the "No data" placeholder. |

| `deviationLow` |  | `number[]` |  |
| `toleranceLow` |  | `number[]` |  |
| `toleranceHigh` |  | `number[]` |  |
| `deviationHigh` |  | `number[]` |  |

### `fclArrows` — function

Arrows of the separators after the begin column ([0]) and after the mid column ([1]) in a layout (UI5 FCL). The second separator's arrows need an end column (`hasEnd`).

```ts
fclArrows(layout: FclLayout, hasEnd: boolean): [FclArrow | undefined, FclArrow | undefined]
```

### `FclLayout` — type

Column layouts and their widths (UI5 sap.f.LayoutType): begin / mid / end in percent.

```ts
export type FclLayout = | 'OneColumn' // 100 | 'TwoColumnsBeginExpanded' // 67 / 33 | 'TwoColumnsMidExpanded' // 33 / 67 | 'ThreeColumnsMidExpanded' // 25 / 50 / 25 | 'ThreeColumnsEndExpanded' // 25 / 25 / 50 | 'ThreeColumnsMidExpandedEndHidden' // 33 / 67 / 0 | 'ThreeColumnsBeginExpandedEndHidden' // 67 / 33 / 0 | 'MidColumnFullScreen' | 'EndColumnFullScreen';
```

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `run` | **yes** | `(action: ActionModel, targets: ActionTarget[]) => void` |  |
| `busy` | **yes** | `boolean` |  |
| `dialogs` | **yes** | `ReactNode` | Dialogs (partial processing, parameters); render once. |

### `ActionTarget` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `row` | **yes** | `Row` |  |
| `path` | **yes** | `string` |  |
| `label` | **yes** | `string` | Short identifier shown in the partial processing dialog. |

### `isActionAvailable` — function

Whether an action can be applied to a row (Core.OperationAvailable; missing means available).

```ts
isActionAvailable(action: ActionModel, row: Row | undefined): boolean
```

### `useActionRunner` — hook

```ts
useActionRunner(o: { service?: string; onDone?(r: ActionResult): void; }): ActionRunner
```

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/edit`

`CreateDialog` (component) · `CreateDialogProps` (interface) · `createFields` (function) · `createWith` (function) · `fieldErrors` (function) · `isEditable` (function) · `useEditStrategy` (hook) · `validate` (function)

### `CreateDialog` — component

Dialog with the fields of a new object (LR Add/Create Items Using a Dialog, OP Create and edit subobjects).

`import { CreateDialog } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/edit';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `opened` | **yes** | `boolean` | Whether the dialog is open (controlled). |
| `onClose` | **yes** | `() => void` | Called when the dialog closes. |
| `title` | **yes** | `string` | Dialog title, e.g. "Create Purchase Order". |
| `typeName` | **yes** | `string` | Full name of the entity type, e.g. "Shop.Order" (`model.entitySets[set].typeName`). |
| `fields` | **yes** | `FieldModel[]` | Fields asked in the dialog (usually from `createFields(...)`: keys that are not computed and mandatory properties). |
| `onCreate` | **yes** | `(values: Row) => Promise&lt;void>` | Creates the object from the values; throws to keep the dialog open (messages are shown in the dialog). |
| `service` |  | `string` | Name of the service in `SmartKitProvider` `services`; default: the provider's `defaultService`. |

### `CreateDialogProps` — interface

Props of `CreateDialog` (see above).

### `createFields` — function

Fields of a create dialog: the mandatory properties (LR Add/Create Using a Dialog: fewer than 8 required fields).

```ts
createFields(model: MetaModel, typeName: string, lineItem: FieldModel[]): FieldModel[]
```

### `createWith` — function

Creates an entity with the edit strategy (draft: create, set, activate; direct: POST).

```ts
createWith(strategy: EditStrategy, target: QuerySource, values: Row): Promise<{ path: string; row: Row; messages: Message[]; }>
```

### `fieldErrors` — function

Field errors by property path from messages.

```ts
fieldErrors(messages: Message[]): Record<string, string>
```

### `isEditable` — function

Whether a property can be changed: not computed, not a key, not read-only (immutable only when creating).

```ts
isEditable(prop: PropertyModel | undefined, creating: boolean): boolean
```

### `useEditStrategy` — hook

The edit strategy of an entity set (service override or the default by model).

```ts
useEditStrategy(entitySet: string, serviceName?: string): EditStrategy
```

### `validate` — function

Client validation (OP In Edit Mode): mandatory fields and number formats; messages target the property path.

```ts
validate(model: MetaModel, typeName: string, fields: FieldModel[], values: Row, t: TextFn, creating?: boolean): Message[]
```

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/templates`

`ListContentItem` (interface) · `SmartListReport` (component) · `SmartListReportProps` (interface) · `SmartObjectPage` (component) · `SmartObjectPageProps` (interface)

### `ListContentItem` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `key` | **yes** | `string` |  |
| `entitySet` |  | `string` | Entity set of the table (multiple content); defaults to the page's entity set. |
| `title` |  | `string` |  |
| `tableExtend` |  | `TableExtendEntry[]` |  |

### `SmartListReport` — component

`import { SmartListReport } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/templates';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `entitySet` | **yes** | `string` | Entity set of the list, e.g. "Orders". Required. Columns come from `UI.LineItem`, filters from `UI.SelectionFields`, the title from `UI.HeaderInfo/TypeNamePlural`. |
| `service` |  | `string` | Name of the service in `SmartKitProvider` `services`; default: the provider's `defaultService`. |
| `pageKey` |  | `string` | Key for variants and state restoration; defaults to the entity set. |
| `liveMode` |  | `boolean` | Live update mode (default true, recommended by the list report guideline): every change filters immediately. False: manual update mode with "Go", for services where filtering on every change is too slow. |
| `autoExecute` |  | `boolean` | Execute the standard variant on load (default true). |
| `title` |  | `string` | Page title; default `UI.HeaderInfo/TypeNamePlural` of the entity type. |
| `headerActions` |  | `ReactNode` | Extra buttons in the page header toolbar. |
| `filterExtend` |  | `ExtendEntry&lt;FilterFieldItem>[]` | Insert, replace or remove filter fields (ids are the property paths). |
| `tableExtend` |  | `TableExtendEntry[]` | Insert, replace or remove columns and toolbar actions (`TableExtendEntry`, ids as in the model). |
| `onRowNavigate` |  | `(row: Row, path: string) => void` | Row press; receives the row and its entity path. Default: `NavigationAdapter.toObject` when the adapter has an object page for the set. |
| `beforeTable` |  | `ReactNode` | Content between header and table (e.g. KPI cards). |
| `filterToggle` |  | `boolean` | "Show Filters / Hide Filters" in the title bar instead of the expand/collapse and pin buttons on the header border (the variant the list report guideline describes for grid tables). Default false: the header features as in the SAP reference app; the header collapses while the table is scrolled down and expands at the top, unless pinned. |
| `className` |  | `string` | Extra CSS class on the root element. |
| `views` |  | `{ mode: 'switch' \| 'tabs'; ids?: string[] }` | Multiple views (LR Multiple Views) from the qualified UI.SelectionPresentationVariant annotations: "switch" replaces the table title area with a segmented button (≤ 3 views) or a select (> 3); "tabs" shows a tab per view. |
| `contents` |  | `ListContentItem[]` | Multiple content (LR Multiple Content): a text-only tab per table, all filtered by the same filter bar. |
| `tableType` |  | `'grid' \| 'responsive' \| 'auto' \| TableType` | Table type: grid (desktop), responsive, or auto = responsive below 600px (LR Responsiveness, docs/12 U-17). The UI5 names (sap.ui.comp.smarttable.TableType) are accepted too: "Table" and "AnalyticalTable" = grid, "ResponsiveTable" = responsive, "TreeTable" = grid tree table of the recursive hierarchy (docs/26 §3.10). |
| `hierarchyQualifier` |  | `string` | Tree tables: qualifier of Aggregation.RecursiveHierarchy (default: the type's first hierarchy). |
| `chart` |  | `boolean \| string` | Offer a chart view from UI.Chart (qualifier; true = the first chart); views use the chart of their presentation variant. |
| `workingMode` |  | `'worklist' \| 'continuous'` | Working mode after actions (LR Working Modes; default worklist). |
| `actionGroups` |  | `ActionGroup[]` | Menu buttons for similar table actions. |
| `share` |  | `boolean` | Share button in the header toolbar (default true when the navigation adapter can build links). |
| `onRowsChange` |  | `(rows: Row[]) => void` | Loaded rows of the main table (e.g. paging buttons in a flexible column layout). |
| `refreshToken` |  | `number \| string` | Change this value to reload the tables, tab counts and chart (after the host changed data elsewhere, e.g. in a drawer or dialog). The first value does not reload. |
| `exportable` |  | `boolean` | Offer "Export to Spreadsheet" in the table toolbars (default true). |
| `onBeforeExport` |  | `(e: ExportEvent) => boolean \| void \| Promise&lt;boolean \| void>` | Before an export is written; return false to cancel (see `SmartTable` `onBeforeExport`). |
| `onExported` |  | `(e: ExportEvent) => void` | After an export was written. |
| `canSaveVariants` |  | `boolean` | Whether views may be saved (default: the service's `VariantStore.canSave`, else true). False: the view menu only selects views (no Save, Save As, Manage). |

### `SmartListReportProps` — interface

Props of `SmartListReport` (see above).

### `SmartObjectPage` — component

`import { SmartObjectPage } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/templates';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `path` | **yes** | `string` | Entity path, e.g. "Orders('1')". Ignored in create mode. |
| `service` |  | `string` | Name of the service in `SmartKitProvider` `services`; default: the provider's `defaultService`. |
| `navigation` |  | `'anchor' \| 'tabs'` | "anchor" (default): sections in one scrolling page with an anchor bar; "tabs": one section at a time. |
| `headerActions` |  | `ReactNode` | Extra buttons in the header toolbar, before the model actions (`UI.Identification` DataFieldForAction). |
| `headerMessage` |  | `ReactNode \| ((row: Row \| undefined) => ReactNode)` | Message strip under the title (OP-17). |
| `sectionExtend` |  | `ExtendEntry&lt;SectionItem>[]` | Insert or replace sections. |
| `tableExtend` |  | `Record&lt;string, TableExtendEntry[]>` | Extensions for the sub-table of a navigation. |
| `extraSelect` |  | `string[]` | Extra paths to read (for custom content such as headerMessage). |
| `onRowNavigate` |  | `(row: Row, path: string) => void` | Navigation of a sub-table row; default: NavigationAdapter.toObject when the adapter has an object page. |
| `className` |  | `string` | Extra CSS class on the root element. |
| `create` |  | `{ entitySet: string; initial?: Row }` | Create mode (OP Create): a new object of this entity set. |
| `onCreated` |  | `(path: string) => void` | After a new object was saved; default NavigationAdapter.toObject. |
| `onDeleted` |  | `() => void` | After the object was deleted; default NavigationAdapter.toList. |
| `editable` |  | `boolean` | Offer "Edit" (default: when the entity set is updatable and the data provider can write). |
| `subObjectCreate` |  | `Record&lt;string, 'inline' \| 'dialog' \| 'none'>` | How sub-objects of a navigation are created while editing (OP Create and edit subobjects). |
| `keyInfo` |  | `ReactNode \| ((row: Row \| undefined) => ReactNode)` | Key information in the title bar (DP Header Title (4)), e.g. a GenericTag. |
| `sideContent` |  | `ReactNode \| ((row: Row \| undefined) => ReactNode)` | Dynamic side content (OP Dynamic Side Content). |
| `sideContentTitle` |  | `string` | Title of the side content panel. |
| `paging` |  | `boolean` | Paging buttons for sub-object pages (OP Representation of Child Pages; default true). |
| `showAllThreshold` |  | `number` | Limit for sub-table previews with "Show All (x)" (default 200). |
| `letterboxing` |  | `boolean` | Letterboxing of the page (DP Responsiveness). |
| `partialHeaderEdit` |  | `boolean` | "Edit Header" for the header fields in a dialog (OP partial edit; default true). Turn it off when the host's `editStrategy` saves the whole page at once; "Edit" still changes the header fields. |
| `share` |  | `boolean` | "Share" in the header actions (default true). |

### `SmartObjectPageProps` — interface

Props of `SmartObjectPage` (see above).

### `$CardSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `header` |  | `ReactNode` | Header (`Header` or `NumericHeader`). |
| `headerPosition` |  | `HeaderPosition` | Position of the header. |
| `content` |  | `ReactNode` | Content (`ListContent`, `TableContent`, …, or any React content: the Component card); the same as `children`. |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode` | The content as JSX children (used when `content` is not given). |
| `footer` |  | `ReactNode` | Footer, e.g. buttons (the integration card's actions strip). |
| `width` |  | `string` | CSS width (the width of the container). |
| `height` |  | `string` | CSS height (the height of the content). |
| `semanticRole` |  | `SemanticRole` | Accessibility role: a region, or an item of a list (then `onPress` makes the whole card interactive). |
| `headingLevel` |  | `TitleLevel` | Heading level of the header title (aria-level). |
| `onPress` |  | `() => void` | Press of the whole card; only with `semanticRole` "ListItem" (sap.f.CardBase#press). |
| `className` |  | `string` | Extra CSS class on the root element. |
| `style` |  | `CSSProperties` | Inline style, e.g. `{ width: 400 }`; the `width` / `height` props win over the style's. |
| `aria-roledescription` |  | `string` | Accessible description of the card type, e.g. "List card". |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/f/cards/Header`

`$HeaderSettings` (interface) · `AvatarColor` (type) · `AvatarImageFitType` (type) · `AvatarShape` (type) · `AvatarSize` (type) · `default` (component)

### `$HeaderSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `title` |  | `string` | Title; default: from the content's annotations (UI.HeaderInfo). |
| `subtitle` |  | `string` | Subtitle text. |
| `iconState` |  | `ValueState` | A status-coloured message icon in the icon area instead of the avatar (do not combine with iconSrc / iconInitials). |
| `statusText` |  | `string` | Counter text, e.g. "5 of 40"; default: from the content (CD-03). |
| `titleMaxLines` |  | `number` | Lines before the title is truncated (CD-02). |
| `subtitleMaxLines` |  | `number` | Lines before the subtitle is truncated (CD-02). |
| `dataTimestamp` |  | `string` | Time of the oldest data in the card (ISO 8601), shown as a relative time such as "5 minutes ago". |
| `wrappingType` |  | `WrappingType` | Wrapping of the title, subtitle and details texts. |
| `infoSection` |  | `ReactNode[]` | Info sections shown at the bottom of the header (`infoSection` aggregation). |
| `toolbar` |  | `ReactNode` | Additional content in the header's toolbar area, e.g. a menu button (`toolbar` aggregation); pressing it does not press the header. |
| `onPress` |  | `() => void` | Press of the header (event `press`). |
| `iconSrc` |  | `string \| ReactElement` | Image URL of the avatar, or an icon element (for example from lucide-react; UI5 takes an icon URI, ADR-032 rule 5). |
| `iconInitials` |  | `string` | Initials shown in the avatar. |
| `iconDisplayShape` |  | `AvatarShape` | Avatar shape. |
| `iconAlt` |  | `string` | Alternative text of the avatar (its accessible name). |
| `iconBackgroundColor` |  | `AvatarColor` | Background colour of the avatar. |
| `iconSize` |  | `AvatarSize` | Size of the avatar ('Custom' is shown as 'S'). |
| `iconFitType` |  | `AvatarImageFitType` | How an image fills the avatar. |

| `dataPointAnnotationPath` |  | `string` | e.g. "com.sap.vocabularies.UI.v1.DataPoint#Spend" or "@UI.DataPoint#Spend". |
| `selectionAnnotationPath` |  | `string` | UI.SelectionVariant that filters the KPI. |
| `filters` |  | `FilterState` | Additional filters (e.g. from a filter bar). |
| `statusText` |  | `string` | Counter text, e.g. "5 of 40"; default: from the content (CD-03). |
| `titleMaxLines` |  | `number` | Lines before the title is truncated (CD-02). |
| `subtitleMaxLines` |  | `number` | Lines before the subtitle is truncated (CD-02). |
| `dataTimestamp` |  | `string` | Time of the oldest data in the card (ISO 8601), shown as a relative time such as "5 minutes ago". |
| `wrappingType` |  | `WrappingType` | Wrapping of the title, subtitle and details texts. |
| `infoSection` |  | `ReactNode[]` | Info sections shown at the bottom of the header (`infoSection` aggregation). |
| `toolbar` |  | `ReactNode` | Additional content in the header's toolbar area, e.g. a menu button (`toolbar` aggregation); pressing it does not press the header. |
| `onPress` |  | `() => void` | Press of the header (event `press`). |
| `iconSrc` |  | `string \| ReactElement` | Image URL of the avatar, or an icon element (for example from lucide-react; UI5 takes an icon URI, ADR-032 rule 5). |
| `iconInitials` |  | `string` | Initials shown in the avatar. |
| `iconDisplayShape` |  | `AvatarShape` | Avatar shape. |
| `iconAlt` |  | `string` | Alternative text of the avatar (its accessible name). |
| `iconBackgroundColor` |  | `AvatarColor` | Background colour of the avatar. |
| `iconSize` |  | `AvatarSize` | Size of the avatar ('Custom' is shown as 'S'). |
| `iconFitType` |  | `AvatarImageFitType` | How an image fills the avatar. |

The custom icon properties shared by BusyIndicator and BusyDialog.

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/DynamicDateRange`

`$DynamicDateRangeSettings` (interface) · `default` (component) · `DynamicDateFormatter` (interface) · `DynamicDateOption` (interface) · `DynamicDateRange$ChangeEvent` (interface) · `DynamicDateRangeValue` (interface)

### `$DynamicDateRangeSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` |  | `DynamicDateRangeValue` | The value (controlled); undefined: empty. |
| `width` |  | `string` | CSS width of the field. |
| `enabled` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `valueState` |  | `ValueState` |  |
| `name` |  | `string` | Name of the input (forms). |
| `placeholder` |  | `string` |  |
| `editable` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `valueStateText` |  | `string` |  |
| `required` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `enableGroupHeaders` |  | `boolean` | Shows the group headers (single dates, date ranges, weeks …) in the option list. |
| `formatter` |  | `DynamicDateFormatter` | Formats and parses the dates in the field (UI5: a DateFormat instance). |
| `standardOptions` |  | `readonly StandardDynamicDateRangeKeys[]` | The standard options offered; the list keeps the UI5 grouping and this order inside each group. Default: all 68 keys (`DEFAULT_STANDARD_OPTIONS`). |
| `hideInput` |  | `boolean` | Renders no field; the popover is opened with `open(domRef)` / `openBy(domRef)`. |
| `calendarWeekNumbering` |  | `CalendarWeekNumbering` | Week numbering for week options (default: the locale). |
| `showClearIcon` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `customOptions` |  | `DynamicDateOption[]` | Own options (sap.m.DynamicDateOption). |
| `onChange` |  | `(e: DynamicDateRange$ChangeEvent) => void` | Fired when the user picks, types or clears a value; `valid` is false when typed text could not be read. |
| `label` |  | `ReactNode` | Extension: field label (Mantine input label). |
| `id` |  | `string` |  |
| `aria-label` |  | `string` |  |
| `ref` |  | `Ref&lt;DynamicDateRange>` |  |

### `DynamicDateFormatter` — interface

The date formatter of the `formatter` property (UI5: a sap.ui.core.format.DateFormat instance).

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `format` | **yes** | `(date: Date) => string` |  |
| `parse` |  | `(text: string) => Date \| null` |  |

### `DynamicDateOption` — interface

A custom option (sap.m.DynamicDateOption); methods are optional except where the option needs them.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `key` | **yes** | `string` |  |
| `valueTypes` |  | `string[]` | The value types, e.g. `['int']`, `['date']`; empty: the option is picked without a detail page. |
| `getText` |  | `() => string` | The option text in the list (UI5 `getText`). |
| `getGroupHeader` |  | `() => string` | The group header shown above the option (UI5 `getGroupHeader`); default "Other Options". |
| `format` |  | `(value: DynamicDateRangeValue) => string` |  |
| `parse` |  | `(text: string) => DynamicDateRangeValue \| null \| undefined` |  |
| `toDates` |  | `(value: DynamicDateRangeValue, calendarWeekNumbering?: CalendarWeekNumbering) => Date[]` |  |
| `createValueHelpUI` |  | `(ctx: { value: DynamicDateRangeValue \| undefined; update(value: DynamicDateRangeValue \| undefined): void }) => ReactNode` | The detail page content. `update` takes the value the page stands for (undefined while it is incomplete); React's answer to UI5's `createValueHelpUI(oControl, fnControlsUpdated)`. |
| `validateValueHelpUI` |  | `(value: DynamicDateRangeValue \| undefined) => boolean` | Whether the detail page is complete (default: a value was given to `update`). |
| `enhanceFormattedValue` |  | `() => boolean` | Whether the field also shows the resolved dates, e.g. "Today (Nov 17, 2026)". |

### `DynamicDateRange$ChangeEvent` — interface

Parameters of the `change` event.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | **yes** | `DynamicDateRangeValue \| undefined` | The current value; undefined when the field was cleared or the text could not be read. |
| `valid` | **yes** | `boolean` | Whether the new value is valid. |

### `DynamicDateRangeValue` — interface

The value of a DynamicDateRange, as in UI5: `{ operator: 'LASTDAYS', values: [7] }`, `{ operator: 'DATE', values: [Date] }`, `{ operator: 'DATERANGE', values: [Date, Date] }`, `{ operator: 'SPECIFICMONTH', values: [monthIndex] }`, `{ operator: 'SPECIFICMONTHINYEAR', values: [monthIndex, year] }`, `{ operator: 'TODAYFROMTO', values: [daysBefore, daysAfter] }`. Custom options use their own key and values.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `operator` | **yes** | `StandardDynamicDateRangeKeys \| (string & {})` |  |
| `values` | **yes** | `Array&lt;Date \| number \| string>` |  |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/IconTabBar`

`$IconTabBarSettings` (interface) · `default` (component) · `IconTabBar$ExpandEvent` (interface) · `IconTabBar$SelectEvent` (interface) · `IconTabFilterItem` (interface) · `IconTabItem` (type)

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `items` |  | `ReactNode` | IconTabFilter and IconTabSeparator elements (UI5 `items`). |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode` | More IconTabFilter and IconTabSeparator elements, after `items` (the JSX form of `items`). |
| `content` |  | `ReactNode` | Content shown for filters that have none of their own (UI5 `content`). |
| `expandable` |  | `boolean` | Pressing the selected tab collapses and expands the content. |
| `expanded` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `selectedKey` |  | `string` | Key of the selected filter; without it the first filter. The bar keeps the user's choice until this prop changes. |
| `upperCase` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `stretchContentHeight` |  | `boolean` | The bar fills the parent's height and the content scrolls. |
| `applyContentPadding` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `backgroundDesign` |  | `BackgroundDesign` | Background of the content. |
| `headerMode` |  | `IconTabHeaderMode` | Inline: text and count on one line. |
| `headerBackgroundDesign` |  | `BackgroundDesign` |  |
| `enableTabReordering` |  | `boolean` | Tabs can be moved by drag and drop and with Ctrl + arrow keys. |
| `maxNestingLevel` |  | `number` | Kept for UI5 compatibility: drag and drop reorders on the top level only. |
| `tabDensityMode` |  | `IconTabDensityMode` |  |
| `ariaTexts` |  | `{ headerLabel?: string; headerDescription?: string }` | Accessible texts of the header (UI5 `ariaTexts`). |
| `tabsOverflowMode` |  | `TabsOverflowMode` | Where the overflow buttons go when the tabs do not fit. |
| `onSelect` |  | `(e: IconTabBar$SelectEvent) => void` |  |
| `onExpand` |  | `(e: IconTabBar$ExpandEvent) => void` |  |

### `default` — component

`import { default } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/IllustratedMessage';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `description` |  | `string` | Description; defaults to the type's text when enableDefaultTitleAndDescription is on. |
| `enableDefaultTitleAndDescription` |  | `boolean` | Uses the default title and description of the type for the missing ones. |
| `enableFormattedText` |  | `boolean` | Accepted for compatibility: the description stays plain text, HTML is not parsed. |
| `enableVerticalResponsiveness` |  | `boolean` | Auto size also follows the container height; the container then needs a height. |
| `illustrationSize` |  | `IllustratedMessageSize` | Size (Auto: chosen by the container width — below 160px Base without illustration, then ExtraSmall, Small, Medium, and from 680px Large). |
| `illustrationType` |  | `IllustratedMessageType` | Illustration; `src` takes precedence. |
| `src` |  | `string` | Illustration as a URI, taking precedence over `illustrationType`: `sap-illustration://&lt;type>` (or `sap-illustration://sapIllus/&lt;type>`) for a type of the default set. Other sets are not available; the control then falls back to `illustrationType`. |
| `title` |  | `string` | Title; defaults to the type's text when enableDefaultTitleAndDescription is on. |
| `ariaTitleLevel` |  | `'Auto' \| 'H1' \| 'H2' \| 'H3' \| 'H4' \| 'H5' \| 'H6'` | sap.ui.core.TitleLevel of the title (Auto renders a level-2 heading). |
| `decorative` |  | `boolean` | The illustration is decorative: hidden from screen readers (false: an image labelled with the title). |
| `additionalContent` |  | `ReactNode` | Actions below the description, usually buttons. |
| `illustrationAriaLabelledBy` |  | `string \| string[]` | IDs of the elements that label the illustration (aria-labelledby on the illustration; ignored when decorative). |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/MessageBox`

`default` (const) · `MessageBoxAction` (type) · `MessageBoxIcon` (type) · `MessageBoxOptions` (interface)

### `default` — const

sap.m.MessageBox. Every method returns a promise of the pressed action (extension: UI5 returns nothing and only calls `onClose`).

```ts
const default: { Action: { readonly OK: "OK"; readonly CANCEL: "CANCEL"; readonly YES: "YES"; readonly NO: "NO"; readonly ABORT: "ABORT"; readonly RETRY…
```

### `MessageBoxAction` — type

sap.m.MessageBox.Action; a custom action is any other string (shown as the button text).

```ts
export type MessageBoxAction = 'OK' | 'CANCEL' | 'YES' | 'NO' | 'ABORT' | 'RETRY' | 'IGNORE' | 'CLOSE' | 'DELETE';
```

### `MessageBoxIcon` — type

sap.m.MessageBox.Icon

```ts
export type MessageBoxIcon = 'NONE' | 'INFORMATION' | 'WARNING' | 'ERROR' | 'SUCCESS' | 'QUESTION';
```

### `MessageBoxOptions` — interface

Options of `MessageBox.show` and the shortcuts (sap.m.MessageBox mOptions, 1.154).

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `icon` |  | `MessageBoxIcon` | Only `show`: the icon (default NONE; INFORMATION when `details` is set). |
| `title` |  | `string` | Dialog title; the shortcuts default to a title per type ("Alert", "Confirmation", "Error" …). |
| `actions` |  | `Array&lt;MessageBoxAction \| (string & {})> \| MessageBoxAction \| (string & {})` | One action or an array: MessageBox.Action values or custom texts. A single (non-array) action is emphasized. |
| `emphasizedAction` |  | `MessageBoxAction \| (string & {}) \| null` | The emphasized action; the shortcuts emphasize OK only when `actions` is not given. null for none. |
| `onClose` |  | `(action: MessageBoxAction \| (string & {}) \| null) => void` | Called with the pressed action, or null when the box was closed with Escape. |
| `id` |  | `string` | Id of the dialog element. |
| `initialFocus` |  | `MessageBoxAction \| (string & {}) \| null` | The action (or button text) whose button gets the initial focus. |
| `details` |  | `string \| object \| (() => Promise&lt;string \| object>)` | Shown after "View Details": a string, an object (as JSON) or a function returning a promise of one. |
| `contentWidth` |  | `string` | CSS width of the dialog content. |
| `verticalScrolling` |  | `boolean` | Scrolling of the content (UI5 default true for both). |
| `horizontalScrolling` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `closeOnNavigation` |  | `boolean` | Accepted and ignored: smart-kit has no router (ADR-010) and no dependent-control lifecycle. |
| `dependentOn` |  | `unknown` |  |
| `styleClass` |  | `string` | Accepted and ignored (ADR-032 rule 7). |
| `textDirection` |  | `string` |  |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/MessageItem`

`$MessageItemSettings` (interface) · `default` (component)

### `$MessageItemSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `type` |  | `MessageType` | Type of the message. |
| `title` |  | `string` |  |
| `subtitle` |  | `string` |  |
| `description` |  | `string` |  |
| `counter` |  | `number` | A number shown at the end of the item (how often the message occurs). |
| `groupName` |  | `string` | The group of the item when the view has `groupItems`. |
| `activeTitle` |  | `boolean` | The title is a link (fires onActiveTitlePress). |
| `markupDescription` |  | `boolean` | UI5 renders the description as HTML; smart-kit renders it as plain text. |
| `longtextUrl` |  | `string` | A URL with a long text, loaded when the details page opens (shown as plain text; fires onLongtextLoaded). |
| `link` |  | `ReactNode` | A link shown on the details page (`link` aggregation). |
| `text` |  | `string` | Text of the item (inherited from sap.ui.core.Item); like UI5, the view shows `title`, not `text`. |
| `enabled` |  | `boolean` | Inherited from sap.ui.core.Item; like UI5, the view does not use it. |

### `default` — component

sap.m.MessageItem: declares one message of a MessageView / MessagePopover (renders nothing on its own).

| `text` |  | `string` | Text of the item (inherited from sap.ui.core.Item); like UI5, the view shows `title`, not `text`. |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/MessagePopover`

`$MessagePopoverSettings` (interface) · `default` (component) · `MessagePopover$ActiveTitlePressEvent` (type) · `MessagePopover$AfterCloseEvent` (interface) · `MessagePopover$AfterOpenEvent` (interface) · `MessagePopover$BeforeCloseEvent` (interface) · `MessagePopover$BeforeOpenEvent` (interface) · `MessagePopover$ItemSelectEvent` (type) · `MessagePopover$ListSelectEvent` (type)

### `$MessagePopoverSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `placement` |  | `VerticalPlacementType` | Position of the popover: below, or above when there is no room ('Vertical'), always 'Top' or 'Bottom'. |
| `initiallyExpanded` |  | `boolean` | Opens with the message list shown; false opens with only the header and filter. |
| `groupItems` |  | `boolean` | Groups the items by `groupName`. |
| `onItemSelect` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$ItemSelectEvent) => void` |  |
| `onListSelect` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$ListSelectEvent) => void` |  |
| `onActiveTitlePress` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$ActiveTitlePressEvent) => void` |  |
| `onBeforeOpen` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$BeforeOpenEvent) => void` |  |
| `onAfterOpen` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$AfterOpenEvent) => void` |  |
| `onBeforeClose` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$BeforeCloseEvent) => void` |  |
| `onAfterClose` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$AfterCloseEvent) => void` |  |
| `aria-label` |  | `string` | Accessible name of the popover (default "Messages"). |
| `ref` |  | `Ref&lt;MessagePopover>` |  |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode` | `MessageItem` elements. |
| `items` |  | `Message[]` | Extension: core messages (ADR-023), shown like MessageItems: text as title, target as subtitle and active title. |
| `asyncDescriptionHandler` |  | `(config: MessageViewAsyncDescriptionConfig) => void` | Loads the description of an item with a `longtextUrl` when its details open (the details page is busy meanwhile). Default: `defaultAsyncDescriptionHandler` (GET `longtextUrl`, as UI5's default handler). |
| `headerButton` |  | `ReactNode` | A button at the end of the list header (MessagePopover `headerButton`). |
| `onLongtextLoaded` |  | `() => void` | Fired after the description of an item with a `longtextUrl` has been loaded (`asyncDescriptionHandler`). |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/MessageToast`

`default` (const) · `MessageToastOptions` (interface)

### `default` — const

```ts
const default: { show(message: ReactNode, o?: MessageToastOptions): void; }
```

### `MessageToastOptions` — interface

sap.m.MessageToast `show` options (1.154).

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `duration` |  | `number` | Milliseconds before the close animation starts (UI5 default 3000). |
| `width` |  | `string` | CSS width (UI5 default 15em; the default comes from the token --sk-toast-width). |
| `my` |  | `Dock` | The point of the toast that is aligned (UI5 default 'CenterBottom'). |
| `at` |  | `Dock` | The point of the reference (`of`) the toast is aligned to (UI5 default 'CenterBottom'). |
| `of` |  | `Element` | The reference element (default: the window). |
| `offset` |  | `string` | Offset 'x y' in pixels (UI5 default '0 0'). |
| `collision` |  | `string` | 'fit', 'flip' or 'none', per axis (UI5 default 'fit fit'); smart-kit always keeps the toast inside the window. |
| `onClose` |  | `() => void` | Called after the toast has closed. |
| `autoClose` |  | `boolean` | Closes as soon as the user touches or clicks the screen (UI5 default true). |
| `animationTimingFunction` |  | `string` | CSS timing function of the close animation (UI5 default 'ease'). |
| `animationDuration` |  | `number` | Duration of the close animation in milliseconds (UI5 default 1000). |
| `closeOnBrowserNavigation` |  | `boolean` | Accepted and ignored: smart-kit has no router (ADR-010). |

### `MessageTypeFilter` — type

The type filter of the list: a message type, or 'All'.

```ts
export type MessageTypeFilter = MessageType | 'All';
```

### `MessageView$ActiveTitlePressEvent` — interface

Event object of `onActiveTitlePress` (sap.m.MessageView#activeTitlePress).

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `item` | **yes** | `MessageViewItem` |  |

### `MessageView$ItemSelectEvent` — interface

Event object of `onItemSelect` (sap.m.MessageView#itemSelect): the item whose details are shown.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `item` | **yes** | `MessageViewItem` |  |
| `messageTypeFilter` | **yes** | `MessageTypeFilter` |  |

### `MessageView$ListSelectEvent` — interface

Event object of `onListSelect` (sap.m.MessageView#listSelect): the list shown, filtered by type or not.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `messageTypeFilter` | **yes** | `MessageTypeFilter` |  |

### `MessageViewAsyncDescriptionConfig` — interface

Argument of `asyncDescriptionHandler` (sap.m.MessageView~asyncDescriptionHandler). The handler loads the description of `item` (a copy of the item), sets `item.description` (UI5: `item.setDescription`) and calls `promise.resolve()`; `promise.reject()` shows the details without it.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `item` | **yes** | `MessageViewItem` |  |
| `promise` | **yes** | `{ resolve(value?: unknown): void; reject(reason?: unknown): void }` |  |

### `MessageViewItem` — type

An item as passed to event handlers: its properties, its React key, and (extension) the core message it came from.

```ts
export type MessageViewItem = $MessageItemSettings & { key?: string; message?: Message };
```

### `ObjectIdentifier$TitlePressEvent` — interface

Event object of ObjectIdentifier's titlePress.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `domRef` | **yes** | `HTMLElement` | The title element. |

| `number` |  | `string \| number` | The number. In Default mode it is shown as given; Currency and Unit modes format it with the locale. |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/ObjectStatus`

`$ObjectStatusSettings` (interface) · `default` (component)

### `$ObjectStatusSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `title` |  | `string` | Label shown before the text as "title:". |
| `text` |  | `string` | Status text. |
| `active` |  | `boolean` | Text and icon are clickable; fires onPress. |
| `reactiveAreaMode` |  | `ReactiveAreaMode` | Overlay enlarges the clickable area of an active status. |
| `state` |  | `ValueState \| IndicationColor` | Colour: a ValueState or an IndicationColor. |
| `inverted` |  | `boolean` | Filled background in the state colour. |
| `icon` |  | `ReactNode` | Icon before the text (a React node, e.g. a lucide icon). |
| `emptyIndicatorMode` |  | `EmptyIndicatorMode` | Shows "–" when text is empty. |
| `stateAnnouncementText` |  | `string` | Text read by screen readers for the state, instead of the default ("Error", "Warning", …). |
| `onPress` |  | `(e: Record&lt;string, never>) => void` | Fired when an active status is pressed. |

| `aria-disabled` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates that the element is perceivable but disabled, so it is not editable or otherwise operable. |
| `aria-dropeffect` |  | `"none" \| "copy" \| "execute" \| "link" \| "move" \| "popup" \| undefined` | Indicates what functions can be performed when a dragged object is released on the drop target. **Deprecated**: in ARIA 1.1 |
| `aria-errormessage` |  | `string \| undefined` | Identifies the element that provides an error message for the object. |
| `aria-expanded` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates whether the element, or another grouping element it controls, is currently expanded or collapsed. |
| `aria-flowto` |  | `string \| undefined` | Identifies the next element (or elements) in an alternate reading order of content which, at the user's discretion, allows assistive technology to override the general default of reading in document source order. |
| `aria-grabbed` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates an element's "grabbed" state in a drag-and-drop operation. **Deprecated**: in ARIA 1.1 |
| `aria-haspopup` |  | `boolean \| "false" \| "true" \| "menu" \| "listbox" \| "tree" \| "grid" \| "dialog" \| undefined` | Indicates the availability and type of interactive popup element, such as menu or dialog, that can be triggered by an element. |
| `aria-hidden` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates whether the element is exposed to an accessibility API. |
| `aria-invalid` |  | `boolean \| "false" \| "true" \| "grammar" \| "spelling" \| undefined` | Indicates the entered value does not conform to the format expected by the application. |
| `aria-keyshortcuts` |  | `string \| undefined` | Indicates keyboard shortcuts that an author has implemented to activate or give focus to an element. |
| `aria-label` |  | `string \| undefined` | Defines a string value that labels the current element. |
| `aria-labelledby` |  | `string \| undefined` | Identifies the element (or elements) that labels the current element. |
| `aria-level` |  | `number \| undefined` | Defines the hierarchical level of an element within a structure. |
| `aria-live` |  | `"off" \| "assertive" \| "polite" \| undefined` | Indicates that an element will be updated, and describes the types of updates the user agents, assistive technologies, and user can expect from the live region. |
| `aria-modal` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates whether an element is modal when displayed. |
| `aria-multiline` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates whether a text box accepts multiple lines of input or only a single line. |
| `aria-multiselectable` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates that the user may select more than one item from the current selectable descendants. |
| `aria-orientation` |  | `"horizontal" \| "vertical" \| undefined` | Indicates whether the element's orientation is horizontal, vertical, or unknown/ambiguous. |
| `aria-owns` |  | `string \| undefined` | Identifies an element (or elements) in order to define a visual, functional, or contextual parent/child relationship between DOM elements where the DOM hierarchy cannot be used to represent the relationship. |
| `aria-placeholder` |  | `string \| undefined` | Defines a short hint (a word or short phrase) intended to aid the user with data entry when the control has no value. A hint could be a sample value or a brief description of the expected format. |
| `aria-posinset` |  | `number \| undefined` | Defines an element's number or position in the current set of listitems or treeitems. Not required if all elements in the set are present in the DOM. |
| `aria-pressed` |  | `boolean \| "false" \| "mixed" \| "true" \| undefined` | Indicates the current "pressed" state of toggle buttons. |
| `aria-readonly` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates that the element is not editable, but is otherwise operable. |
| `aria-relevant` |  | `\| "additions" \| "additions removals" \| "additions text" \| "all" \| "removals" \| "removals additions" \| "removals text" \| "text" \| "text ad…` | Indicates what notifications the user agent will trigger when the accessibility tree within a live region is modified. |
| `aria-required` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates that user input is required on the element before a form may be submitted. |
| `aria-roledescription` |  | `string \| undefined` | Defines a human-readable, author-localized description for the role of an element. |
| `aria-rowcount` |  | `number \| undefined` | Defines the total number of rows in a table, grid, or treegrid. |

| `aria-flowto` |  | `string \| undefined` | Identifies the next element (or elements) in an alternate reading order of content which, at the user's discretion, allows assistive technology to override the general default of reading in document source order. |
| `aria-grabbed` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates an element's "grabbed" state in a drag-and-drop operation. **Deprecated**: in ARIA 1.1 |

| `aria-hidden` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates whether the element is exposed to an accessibility API. |
| `aria-invalid` |  | `boolean \| "false" \| "true" \| "grammar" \| "spelling" \| undefined` | Indicates the entered value does not conform to the format expected by the application. |
| `aria-keyshortcuts` |  | `string \| undefined` | Indicates keyboard shortcuts that an author has implemented to activate or give focus to an element. |
| `aria-label` |  | `string \| undefined` | Defines a string value that labels the current element. |
| `aria-labelledby` |  | `string \| undefined` | Identifies the element (or elements) that labels the current element. |
| `aria-level` |  | `number \| undefined` | Defines the hierarchical level of an element within a structure. |
| `aria-live` |  | `"off" \| "assertive" \| "polite" \| undefined` | Indicates that an element will be updated, and describes the types of updates the user agents, assistive technologies, and user can expect from the live region. |
| `aria-modal` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates whether an element is modal when displayed. |
| `aria-multiline` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates whether a text box accepts multiple lines of input or only a single line. |
| `aria-multiselectable` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates that the user may select more than one item from the current selectable descendants. |
| `aria-orientation` |  | `"horizontal" \| "vertical" \| undefined` | Indicates whether the element's orientation is horizontal, vertical, or unknown/ambiguous. |
| `aria-owns` |  | `string \| undefined` | Identifies an element (or elements) in order to define a visual, functional, or contextual parent/child relationship between DOM elements where the DOM hierarchy cannot be used to represent the relationship. |
| `aria-placeholder` |  | `string \| undefined` | Defines a short hint (a word or short phrase) intended to aid the user with data entry when the control has no value. A hint could be a sample value or a brief description of the expected format. |
| `aria-posinset` |  | `number \| undefined` | Defines an element's number or position in the current set of listitems or treeitems. Not required if all elements in the set are present in the DOM. |

`$OverflowToolbarSettings` (interface) · `default` (component) · `OverflowToolbar$PressEvent` (interface) · `OverflowToolbarLayoutData` (interface)

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `width` |  | `string` | Width (CSS length; default 100%). |
| `active` |  | `boolean` | The whole toolbar is clickable and fires onPress. |
| `enabled` |  | `boolean` | Enabled; a disabled toolbar disables its content. |
| `height` |  | `string` | Height (CSS length; default: the control height). |

| `aria-errormessage` |  | `string \| undefined` | Identifies the element that provides an error message for the object. |

| `aria-flowto` |  | `string \| undefined` | Identifies the next element (or elements) in an alternate reading order of content which, at the user's discretion, allows assistive technology to override the general default of reading in document source order. |

| `aria-hidden` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates whether the element is exposed to an accessibility API. |
| `aria-invalid` |  | `boolean \| "false" \| "true" \| "grammar" \| "spelling" \| undefined` | Indicates the entered value does not conform to the format expected by the application. |
| `aria-keyshortcuts` |  | `string \| undefined` | Indicates keyboard shortcuts that an author has implemented to activate or give focus to an element. |
| `aria-label` |  | `string \| undefined` | Defines a string value that labels the current element. |
| `aria-labelledby` |  | `string \| undefined` | Identifies the element (or elements) that labels the current element. |
| `aria-level` |  | `number \| undefined` | Defines the hierarchical level of an element within a structure. |

### `default` — component

Instance methods of OverflowToolbar.

`import { default } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/OverflowToolbar';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `width` |  | `string` | Width (CSS length; default 100%). |
| `active` |  | `boolean` | The whole toolbar is clickable and fires onPress. |
| `enabled` |  | `boolean` | Enabled; a disabled toolbar disables its content. |
| `height` |  | `string` | Height (CSS length; default: the control height). |
| `design` |  | `ToolbarDesign` | Background design (Auto: transparent). |
| `style` |  | `ToolbarStyle` | Clear removes the border. |
| `ariaHasPopup` |  | `AriaHasPopup` | aria-haspopup of an active toolbar. |
| `asyncMode` |  | `boolean` | Accepted for compatibility; the layout is always computed synchronously. |
| `onPress` |  | `(e: OverflowToolbar$PressEvent) => void` | Fired when an active toolbar is pressed outside its interactive content; `srcControl` is the clicked element. |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode` | Toolbar content: buttons, texts, ToolbarSpacer; `layoutData` on a child sets its overflow priority. |

| `aria-haspopup` |  | `boolean \| "false" \| "true" \| "menu" \| "listbox" \| "tree" \| "grid" \| "dialog" \| undefined` | Indicates the availability and type of interactive popup element, such as menu or dialog, that can be triggered by an element. |
| `aria-hidden` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates whether the element is exposed to an accessibility API. |
| `aria-invalid` |  | `boolean \| "false" \| "true" \| "grammar" \| "spelling" \| undefined` | Indicates the entered value does not conform to the format expected by the application. |
| `aria-keyshortcuts` |  | `string \| undefined` | Indicates keyboard shortcuts that an author has implemented to activate or give focus to an element. |
| `aria-label` |  | `string \| undefined` | Defines a string value that labels the current element. |
| `aria-labelledby` |  | `string \| undefined` | Identifies the element (or elements) that labels the current element. |
| `aria-level` |  | `number \| undefined` | Defines the hierarchical level of an element within a structure. |

| `aria-modal` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates whether an element is modal when displayed. |
| `aria-multiline` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` | Indicates whether a text box accepts multiple lines of input or only a single line. |

| `aria-placeholder` |  | `string \| undefined` | Defines a short hint (a word or short phrase) intended to aid the user with data entry when the control has no value. A hint could be a sample value or a brief description of the expected format. |

### `OverflowToolbar$PressEvent` — interface

Event object of OverflowToolbar's press.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `srcControl` | **yes** | `HTMLElement` | The clicked element. |

### `OverflowToolbarLayoutData` — interface

sap.m.OverflowToolbarLayoutData: set it on a toolbar child as the `layoutData` prop.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `priority` |  | `OverflowToolbarPriority` | When the child moves into the overflow (default High). |
| `group` |  | `number` | Children with the same non-zero group move into the overflow together (default 0). |
| `closeOverflowOnInteraction` |  | `boolean` | Pressing the child in the overflow closes the overflow (default true). |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/OverflowToolbarButton`

`$OverflowToolbarButtonSettings` (interface) · `default` (component)

### `$OverflowToolbarButtonSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `text` | **yes** | `string` | Text: the tooltip and accessible name in the toolbar, shown next to the icon in the overflow. |
| `icon` |  | `ReactNode` | Icon (a React node). |
| `type` |  | `ButtonType` | Visual type; Default and Transparent look transparent in the toolbar, as in the SAPUI5 toolbar context. |
| `enabled` |  | `boolean` | Enabled. |
| `width` |  | `string` | Width (CSS length). |
| `iconFirst` |  | `boolean` | In the overflow: the icon before the text; false puts it after. |
| `activeIcon` |  | `ReactNode` | Icon shown while the button is pressed (held down). |
| `ariaHasPopup` |  | `AriaHasPopup` | aria-haspopup. |
| `accessibleRole` |  | `ButtonAccessibleRole` | Link makes screen readers announce a link. |
| `badgeStyle` |  | `BadgeStyle` | Style of the badge. |
| `badge` |  | `string \| number` | Extension: badge value shown on the button (SAPUI5 sets it through BadgeCustomData). |
| `layoutData` |  | `OverflowToolbarLayoutData` | Layout data in the OverflowToolbar. |
| `onPress` |  | `(e: Record&lt;string, never>) => void` | Fired when the button is pressed. |
| `title` |  | `string` | Tooltip (the SAPUI5 tooltip of sap.ui.core.Element); defaults to `text` in the toolbar. |
| `data-testid` |  | `string` |  |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/WizardStep`

`$WizardStepSettings` (interface) · `default` (component)

### `$WizardStepSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` |  | `string` | Step id (used by `nextStep`, `subsequentSteps`, `goToStep`); default: the React key, then the position. |
| `title` |  | `string` |  |
| `icon` |  | `ReactNode` |  |
| `validated` |  | `boolean` | Whether the user may go on to the next step. |
| `optional` |  | `boolean` | Adds "(Optional)" to the title. |
| `nextStep` |  | `string` | With `enableBranching`: the id of the next step (one of `subsequentSteps`). |
| `subsequentSteps` |  | `string[]` | With `enableBranching`: the ids of the steps that can follow. |
| `onActivate` |  | `() => void` | Fired when the step becomes the progress step. |
| `onComplete` |  | `() => void` | Fired when the user goes on from this step. |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode` |  |

### `default` — component

A step of a Wizard; rendered by the Wizard (alone it renders nothing).

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `nextStep` |  | `string` | With `enableBranching`: the id of the next step (one of `subsequentSteps`). |
| `subsequentSteps` |  | `string[]` | With `enableBranching`: the ids of the steps that can follow. |
| `onActivate` |  | `() => void` | Fired when the step becomes the progress step. |
| `onComplete` |  | `() => void` | Fired when the user goes on from this step. |

### `$UploadSetwithTableSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `items` |  | `UploadItem[]` |  |
| `fileTypes` |  | `string[]` | Allowed extensions without dot, case-insensitive. |
| `maxFileNameLength` |  | `number` |  |
| `maxFileSize` |  | `number` | Maximum file size in MB. |
| `mediaTypes` |  | `string[]` | Allowed media types ("image/png", also "image/*"). |
| `uploadUrl` |  | `string` | URL of the implicit uploader (without `uploader`). |
| `httpRequestMethod` |  | `UploaderHttpRequestMethod` | Method of the implicit uploader (without `uploader`). |
| `multiple` |  | `boolean` | Several files at once. |
| `uploadButtonInvisible` |  | `boolean` | Hides the upload button; drag and drop still works. |
| `uploadEnabled` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `itemValidationHandler` |  | `(info: UploadSetwithTableItemInfo) => Promise&lt;UploadItem>` | Called for each new item before its upload; resolve (with the item to upload) to go on, reject to skip it. |
| `directory` |  | `boolean` | Selects folders instead of files. |
| `fileNameValidationConfig` |  | `FilenameValidationConfig` |  |
| `uploader` |  | `ReactElement&lt;$UploaderTableItemSettings>` | The uploader: an `&lt;UploaderTableItem … />` element (default: an implicit one with `uploadUrl` and `httpRequestMethod`). |
| `headerFields` |  | `UploadHeaderField[]` |  |
| `noDataIllustration` |  | `ReactNode` | Empty state; default an IllustratedMessage "UploadToCloud". |
| `actions` |  | `ReactNode` | Toolbar content; an `&lt;ActionsPlaceholder placeholderFor="UploadButtonPlaceholder" />` places the upload button (default: at the end). |
| `previewDialog` |  | `FilePreviewDialog` |  |
| `title` |  | `string` | Extension: title of the toolbar and the table. |
| `columns` |  | `UploadSetwithTableColumn[]` | Extension: columns after the file name, size and status. |
| `onRemove` |  | `(e: UploadSetwithTableRemoveEvent) => void` | Extension: shows a remove button per row. |
| `onItemRenamed` |  | `(e: UploadSetwithTable$ItemRenamedEvent) => void` |  |
| `onItemRenameCanceled` |  | `(e: UploadSetwithTable$ItemRenameCanceledEvent) => void` |  |

| `onUploadCompleted` |  | `(e: UploadSetwithTable$UploadCompletedEvent) => void` |  |
| `onFileTypeMismatch` |  | `(e: UploadSetwithTable$FileTypeMismatchEvent) => void` |  |
| `onFileNameLengthExceeded` |  | `(e: UploadSetwithTable$FileNameLengthExceededEvent) => void` |  |
| `onFileSizeExceeded` |  | `(e: UploadSetwithTable$FileSizeExceededEvent) => void` |  |
| `onMediaTypeMismatch` |  | `(e: UploadSetwithTable$MediaTypeMismatchEvent) => void` |  |
| `onBeforeInitiatingItemUpload` |  | `(e: UploadSetwithTable$BeforeInitiatingItemUploadEvent) => void` |  |
| `onOnActivated` |  | `(e: UploadSetwithTable$OnActivatedEvent) => void` | UI5 event `onActivated`: fired when the control is mounted. |

### `UploadItemInstance` — type

An UploadItem in event objects and ref methods, with the UploadItem methods.

```ts
export type UploadItemInstance = UploadItem & { id: string; /** Downloads the file (`askForLocation`: a save dialog where the browser has one). */ download(askForLocation?: boolean): void; /** The item fails `fileTypes`, `mediaTypes`, `maxFileNameLength` or `maxFileSize`. */ isRestricted(): boolean; /** Aborts a running upload. */ terminateUpload(): void; };
```
